## 2026-10-21 — Strict APK nested Chaquopy binary verification correction

Run [#39522598942](https://github.com/shahvikask/kosha/actions/runs/39522598942) at exact SHA `07070745fff11cfee30e9bcce211b7a4a44e7f50` completed **FAILURE**. The full `:core:model:test :core:database:test :app:assembleDebug :app:assembleDebugAndroidTest` Gradle step **SUCCEEDED** with both APKs built. Original JUnit XML downloaded from artifact `12311733666`: model 70 + database 58 = **128 tests, 0 failures/errors/skips**. The failed shell `grep` searched only the outer APK entry list and therefore did not find Python modules packaged inside Chaquopy's `assets/chaquopy/requirements-{arm64-v8a,x86_64}.imy` nested ZIPs. Independent inspection of the exact original APK artifact proved that both nested ABI archives contain CPython 3.13 `pydantic_core/_pydantic_core` and `pypdfium2_raw/libpdfium.so` ELF binaries with correct machine codes (ARM64 183, x86_64 62); the common archive contains both pinned package metadata entries. This was an **evidence-check defect**, not a failed Android compiler or missing-wheel dependency.

Minimal workflow-only correction: retain strict full Gradle build and both APK existence checks; replace outer-APK greps with nested Chaquopy archive inspection, pinned version metadata checks, ELF magic/ABI checks, and per-binary/APK SHA256 evidence. No tests were weakened or skipped. The strict workflow has an existing branch-specific `on.push` filter for changes to its own file and must be observed on the **new** SHA. No release or emulator/device/owner acceptance. Frozen legacy files and parser remain unchanged. P01–P34 remain SOURCE PARTIAL/NOT ACCEPTED until all gates pass.

## 2026-10-21 — GUDNAT01 full-table typed native archive source and isolated candidate restore (P03/P04/P28/P29)

**Implementation:** `android-native/core/database/.../NativeV9Archive.kt` defines separate `GUDNAT01` magic from legacy `GUDVLT01`/`KOSHA1`, authenticated AES-256-GCM envelope with authenticated 40-byte header, 600,000-round PBKDF2-HMAC-SHA256, random salt16/nonce12, normalized owner email and distinct user-supplied backup secret, 64MiB bounded typed binary snapshot. Includes all 18 canonical v9 tables plus additional schema/table metadata, column declaration/NULL/default/PK and exact SQL storage classes (NULL/INTEGER/TEXT/BLOB/REAL raw IEEE bits); source snapshot SQL is **data**, NEVER executed on decrypt. `NativeV9SnapshotReader.kt` captures actual read-only SQLCipher records inside current device-admitted/password-bound session, checks SQLCipher/FK/quick_check/page-HMAC and never writes plaintext to disk. `NativeV9CandidateRestorer.kt` restores into an externally created **EMPTY UNPUBLISHED independent-key candidate only** after authenticated decrypt, demands source/target schema equality, defers FKs, inserts all tables in one rollback-capable SQL transaction, re-reads and compares every typed cell before committing; wiped transient decoded buffers.
